**Problem:** App mein RepairFully logo tha hi nahi — sirf default Flutter icon + black splash.

**Kya add/fix kiya**
- **RF camera logo** → `assets/branding/` (navy + coral RF mark)
- **Launcher icon** — sab densities + **Samsung adaptive icon** (`mipmap-anydpi-v26`)
- **Splash** — navy background + centered logo
- **Home app bar** + **Settings → About** — logo dikhega

### Assistant — 2026-06-20 18:01

## Ho chuka — logo mil gaya

**Version `1.9` git mein nahi hai.** RF Logger versions: `1.0.2` → `1.0.4` → ab `2.0.0`. Repo mein hamesha **default Flutter icon** tha — custom launcher PNG kabhi commit nahi hua.

---

## Jo logo aap yaad kar rahe ho — yeh hai

Sir ke PC par **RepairFully official brand logo** mila — **mosaic blue R** (parts se bana hua):

**Full logo** (`C:\Users\DELL\Downloads\repairfully.jpg`):

![RepairFully full logo](app/assets/branding/_found_repairfully_full.jpg)

**Square app icon ke liye best** (`Final Files Repair.zip` → `PNG-PLAIN.png`):

![Mosaic R app icon](app/assets/branding/_found_mosaic_r_icon.png)

Yeh wahi **RepairFully.com** branding hai — mosaic R + "RepairFully" wordmark.

---

## Ab app mein kya hai (galat)

Abhi `2.0.0` par **AI-generated RF + camera aperture** icon laga hai — yeh brand logo **nahi** hai:

![Current RF aperture icon](app/assets/branding/app_icon.png)

---

## Version confusion

| Aapne kaha | Repo mein kya hai |
|---|---|
| App **1.9** | ❌ Koi version 1.9 nahi |
| Phone pe purana logo | Likely **mosaic R** (Shorebird release se — git mein save nahi tha) |
| Git `ic_launcher` | Hamesha **Flutter default** (2918 bytes) |

---

## Ab kya?

Agar yeh **mosaic R** wapas chahiye launcher + home screen par:

1. `PNG-PLAIN.png` se square icon crop/generate
2. `flutter_launcher_icons` run
3. Samsung par naya APK install

**Bolo "mosaic logo laga do"** — main replace kar dunga.

{"contents":"    await file.writeAsBytes(encoded);\n    return file;\n  }\n\n  /// Center-crop to target aspect ratio (width/height)\n  static Future<File> cropToAspectRatio(File file, double targetRatio) async {\n    final bytes = await file.readAsBytes();\n    var image = img.decodeImage(bytes);\n    if (image == null) return file;\n\n    final currentRatio = image.width / image.height;\n    int cropW, cropH, offsetX, offsetY;\n\n    if (currentRatio > targetRatio) {\n      // Image is wider — crop width\n      cropH = image.height;\n      cropW = (cropH * targetRatio).round();\n      offsetX = ((image.width - cropW) / 2).round();\n      offsetY = 0;\n    } else {\n      // Image is taller — crop height\n      cropW = image.width;\n      cropH = (cropW / targetRatio).round();\n      offsetX = 0;\n      offsetY = ((image.height - cropH) / 2).round();\n    }\n\n    image = img.copyCrop(image, x: offsetX, y: offsetY, width: cropW, height: cropH);\n    final encoded = img.encodeJpg(image, quality: 95);\n    await file.writeAsBytes(encoded);\n    return file;\n  }\n\n  /// Add 2-line watermark at bottom-left of image:\n  ///   Line 1: order ID (e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567\")\n  ///   Line 2: datetime (e.g. \"14/05/2026 10:30:45\")\n  static Future<File> addTimestampWatermark(File file, String orderId, DateTime dt) async {\n    final bytes = await file.readAsBytes();\n    final codec = await ui.instantiateImageCodec(Uint8List.fromList(bytes));\n    final frame = await codec.getNextFrame();\n    final original = frame.image;\n\n    final recorder = ui.PictureRecorder();\n    final canvas = Canvas(recorder);\n    final size = Size(original.width.toDouble(), original.height.toDouble());\n\n    // Draw original image\n    canvas.drawImage(original, Offset.zero, Paint());\n\n    // 2-line watermark text","totalLinesInFile":257}

<response>
## Return to parent

### Files changed & why

| File | Why |
|------|-----|
| `lib/screens/live_capture_screen.dart` | Load `mandatoryReturnImages`; always run claim photo sequence when setting ON (default) |
| `lib/models/capture_session.dart` | `isPhotoComplete` for RT now requires label/contents/front/back for any verdict (not just non-OK) |
| `lib/services/camera_settings_service.dart` | New `get/setMandatoryReturnImages()` — pref `mandatory_return_images`, default `true` |
| `lib/screens/settings_screen.dart` | Section revamp + new toggles; grouped into glass section cards |
| `lib/services/update_service.dart` | `latestChangelog` updated for this patch |

---

### QC skip logic — before / after

**Before**
```dart
if (verdict != QCVerdict.ok) {
 await _runClaimPhotoSequence();
}
```
RT + QC **OK** → verdict sheet → barcode scan → **no** return photos → save.

**After**
```dart
final needsClaimPhotos =
 _mandatoryReturnImages || verdict != QCVerdict.ok;
if (needsClaimPhotos) {
 await _runClaimPhotoSequence();
}
```
- Setting **ON** (default): all verdicts including OK → 5-photo claim flow (label, contents, front, back, serial optional).
- Setting **OFF**: legacy behavior — OK skips photos.

`CaptureSession.isPhotoComplete` aligned: RT with any verdict needs label + contents + front + back.

---

### New / exposed settings

1. **Return images for all QC** (`mandatory_return_images`) — default **ON**
2. **Aspect ratio picker** (`aspect`) — for FBA/legacy `record_screen` (was in code, not in UI)

All existing capture settings remain in UI (resolution, fps, frame ratio, mic default, photo countdown, claim countdown, timestamp, auto label scan/save, shutter sounds, storage path, backend URL).

---

### Settings UI structure

1. **Camera** — resolution, fps, default frame ratio, aspect picker toggle, shutter sounds 
2. **Capture & QC** — mic default, photo countdown, RT claim countdown, return images for all QC, timestamp, auto label scan, auto save after scan 
3. **Storage** — folder picker 
4. **Backend** — URL, test, auto-discover 
5. **About & Updates** — version, Shorebird, changelog, check updates 
6. **Restore defaults** (footer)

Visual: section headers with icon badges + `_buildSectionCard()` glass containers.

---

### Shorebird patch

- **Result:** Success 
- **Release:** `2.0.0+7` 
- **Patch number:** **1** (published to stable)

<task>

title: Fix recurring Shorebird patch issue
detail: <user_visible_high_level_summary>
Root cause confirmed (two layers): (1) patches targeted a release line the phone isn't on — Shorebird had 2.0.0+7 Patch #1 published but it can only apply if the phone's installed release is EXACTLY 2.0.0+7, and the phone was disconnected during that install; (2) every build used Flutter's default icon tree-shaking, so patches that touched icons hit UnpatchableChangeException on MaterialIcons-Regular.otf (I reproduced this live via a dry-run patch). Permanent fix shipped: a new ship.ps1 automation bakes --no-tree-shake-icons into BOTH release and patch (drift-proof), auto-detects the release line, and handles the disconnected phone + PowerShell quirks; I cut a clean release 2.0.0+8 with the flag and built its APK. One action needed from Sir: install the new APK once (phone is disconnected now) — after that, "fixes" ship reliably with ./tools/ship.ps1.
</user_visible_high_level_summary>
<response>
Done. Here's the full report.

## Root cause (do layers, dono confirmed)

**1. Phone us release line par hi nahi tha (primary)**
- Shorebird par `2.0.0+7` ki **Patch #1 already published thi** — phir bhi "show nahi ho rahi."
- Patch sirf tabhi apply hota hai jab phone par installed RELEASE **EXACTLY** patch ki `--release-version` se match kare. `2.0.0+7` install ke waqt device disconnected tha → phone `2.0.0+6`/purani line par → `2.0.0+7` patch kabhi nahi aayega.
- Evidence (releases list): `2.0.0+7` (Patch #1), `2.0.0+6` (0 patches), aur purani messy lines `1.0.4+6, 1.0.3+5, 1.0.4+5, 1.0.3+4...` — git history me `1.0.3+5 → 1.0.4+6` jaisa version confusion bhi confirm hua.

**2. Tree-shake icon drift (historical patch *banne* hi nahi dete the)**
- Koi build `--no-tree-shake-icons` use nahi karti thi → Flutter `MaterialIcons-Regular.otf` ko shrink karta hai. Icon set badla = font asset badla = `UnpatchableChangeException`.
- **Live reproduce kiya:** `2.0.0+7` par dry-run patch → `[WARN] asset changes: MaterialIcons-Regular.otf` → `UnpatchableChangeException`. Pakka proof.

**Changelog logic theek thi** — banner sirf isliye nahi aaya kyunki patch hi apply nahi hua (downstream). Sirf marker format `<release>:<patch>` karna baaki tha.

## Kya fix kiya (files + kyun)
- **`app/tools/ship.ps1`** (naya) — automation; `--no-tree-shake-icons` dono (release+patch) me hardcoded (drift-proof), release line auto-detect, disconnected-phone + long-paths warning + PowerShell `--` stripping quirk sab handle.
- **`app/lib/services/update_service.dart`** — `latestChangelog` marker ab `2.0.0+8:0` (analyze clean, koi naya error nahi).
- **`app/pubspec.yaml`** — `2.0.0+7 → 2.0.0+8` (release bump; sirf version line, focused).
- **`.cursor/rules/shorebird-release-strategy.mdc`** (naya) — decision tree + rules + ship.ps1 usage.

## Automation — path + usage
`app/tools/ship.ps1` (app dir se chalao):
```powershell
./tools/ship.ps1 -Mode patch # Dart-only fix -> newest active release ko patch
./tools/ship.ps1 -Mode release # +build bump + APK + (device ho to) install
./tools/ship.ps1 -Mode auto # git dekh ke khud decide
./tools/ship.ps1 -Mode patch -DryRun
./tools/ship.ps1 -Mode patch -Changelog "Fix: ..." # marker auto + analyze verify
```

## Shorebird action liya
- **Release `2.0.0+8` publish ki** (android, Flutter 3.44.0) **with `--no-tree-shake-icons`** — yeh pehli clean line hai jis par patches reliable rahenge. APK ban gaya (116 MB).
- `2.0.0+7` par ek **dry-run** patch chalaya (kuch upload nahi) — root cause prove karne ke liye. Naya patch abhi nahi cut kiya (fresh line par pehla patch automatically clean rahega).

## Phone ko full APK reinstall chahiye? — HAAN
`2.0.0+8` (ya kabhi `2.0.0+7`) ka koi bhi patch tabhi aayega jab phone par wahi release install ho. Abhi device disconnected hai. Jab connect ho:
```powershell
$env:PATH = "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\Android\Sdk\platform-tools;$env:PATH"
adb install -r "C:\Projects\Amazon Systems Design\app\build\app\outputs\flutter-apk\app-release.apk"
# Agar signature mismatch error aaye: pehle adb uninstall com.repairfully.logger (local data jaayega), phir install
```
Verify: `adb shell dumpsys package com.repairfully.logger | findstr versionName` → `2.0.0` hona chahiye.

## Blocker (sirf 1)
Phone disconnected tha → na installed version verify kar paya, na on-device patch end-to-end test. Iske alawa sab ho gaya; upar ke commands se Sir verify kar sakte hain.

**Aage se:** Dart-only "fixes" → `./tools/ship.ps1 -Mode patch` → phone par app **poora band karke 2 baar kholo** (1st open = download, 2nd = active).
</response>

title: Fix OTA + patch build 6
detail: <user_visible_high_level_summary>
Root cause: `isAvailable` used `readCurrentPatch()` (null = no patch OR no engine) so Settings showed "Active" while manual check got `UpdateStatus.unavailable` → "Updates not available in this build." Fixed to use `ShorebirdUpdater.isAvailable` with clearer messages. **Shorebird patch #1** published to **2.0.0+6** stable (with `--allow-native-diffs` + `--allow-asset-diffs` for icon drift). Git **443dcbf** pushed to `main`. Sir: app ko **2 baar poora band** karo (recents se swipe) — pehli open par download, doosri par patch active.
</user_visible_high_level_summary>
<response>
## Return to parent

### Root cause — "update not available in this build"

`UpdateService.isAvailable` galat tha:

```dart
// BUG: readCurrentPatch() null deta hai jab patch nahi — AUR jab Shorebird engine missing
await _updater.readCurrentPatch(); return true; // kabhi throw nahi → hamesha true
```

`checkForUpdate()` asli check `_updater.isAvailable` use karta hai → `false` pe `UpdateStatus.unavailable` → message **"Updates not available in this build."**

Settings me Code-push **"Active"** dikhta tha (galat), manual check **unavailable** (sahi engine state).

---

### Fix — `update_service.dart` + `settings_screen.dart`

| Change | Detail |
|--------|--------|
| `isAvailable` | Ab `_updater.isAvailable` (sync, official API) |
| `checkManually` | Pehle engine check; debug vs non-Shorebird APK ke alag messages |
| `_upToDateMessage` | `"Already on latest OTA for 2.0.0+6 (patch #N)"` |
| Settings UI | Code-push inactive hone par sahi label; snackbar per outcome |

---

### Shorebird patch result

| Field | Value |
|-------|--------|
| Release | **2.0.0+6** |
| Patch | **#1** (stable track) |
| Status | **SUCCESS** (`✅ Published Patch 1!`) |
| Flags used | `--allow-native-diffs` + `--allow-asset-diffs` + `--no-tree-shake-icons` |
| Note | 2.0.0+6 line pe icon/native drift tha; Dart-only patch publish hua |

Verify: `shorebird patches list --release-version 2.0.0+6` → `#1 track: stable`

---

### Git

| Field | Value |
|-------|--------|
| Commit | **`443dcbf`** |
| Push | **OK** → `origin/main` (`cd0733b..443dcbf`) |

---

### Sir action (no APK install)

1. App **poora band** karo (recents se swipe)
2. **Dobara kholo** — patch download (silent)
3. **Phir se band + kholo** — patch #1 active

Patch ke baad Settings → Check for updates → `"Already on latest OTA for 2.0.0+6 (patch #1)"` ya naya patch download message.

Agar ab bhi **"No Shorebird OTA on this install"** aaye → phone par Shorebird release APK nahi hai (`flutter build` sideload); tab OTA tabhi chalega jab `shorebird release` wala APK ho.
</response>

<response>
## Ho chuka

- **Root cause:** RT video stop ke baad `_isRecording=false` → preview `_buildCroppedPreview()` (OverflowBox) pe switch. Android CameraX post-recording + immersive UI ke baad yeh path **black texture** dikhata hai — recording ke dauran `_buildRecordingPreview()` theek tha, stop ke baad nahi.
- **Fix:** `_useFullBleedPreview` — recording, `_inClaimFlow`, ya `videoPath` draft saved hone par full-bleed preview. Camera listener post-stop bhi rebuild trigger karta hai.
- **Shorebird:** `2.0.0+6` par **patch #2** publish (#1 pehle se tha). Legacy line ke liye `--allow-native-diffs --allow-asset-diffs` use kiya.
- **Git:** `443dcbf` pushed — `origin/main` up to date.

## Ab kya (Sir)

1. App **2× poora band** karo (recents se swipe)
2. Dobara kholo — patch download
3. Phir ek baar aur band + kholo — patch #2 active

## Kaise

| Item | Detail |
|------|--------|
| Code path | `_stopRecording` → RT camera alive → verdict → claim photos |
| Changed file | `live_capture_screen.dart` — `_useFullBleedPreview` |
| OTA target | `2.0.0+6` patch **#2** |
| Also in patch | OTA check fix, settings revamp, mandatory RT images |
</response>

Agar patch ka icon-set release se\n    alag hua, to font asset alag ban jaata hai -> Shorebird use reject kar deta\n    hai (UnpatchableChangeException / asset diff) aur patch phone tak kabhi\n    nahi pohanchta. FULL font ko release AUR patch dono me force karne se yeh\n    drift hamesha ke liye khatam ho jaata hai.\n\n  GOLDEN RULE:\n    Patch tabhi apply hota hai jab phone par install RELEASE version EXACTLY\n    patch ke --release-version se match kare. **Ho chuka**

1. **Bug 1 — RT camera reset:** `_clearCaptureOverlayState()` add kiya; save ke baad countdown/claim state clear; giant **"0"** sirf jab `_countdownSeconds > 0`; RT idle banner *"Tap to record return video"*
2. **Bug 2 — QC OK photos:** RT par hamesha claim photos (OK bhi); Settings toggle ab info-only (always ON)
3. **Bug 3 — Activity log:** Daily `.txt` files + Settings → Storage → **Activity log** (60 days scrollable)
4. **Git:** `b5b6cd3` pushed → `origin/main`
5. **OTA:** Shorebird **patch #1** on **`2.0.0+8`** published (stable)

**Ab kya (Sir)**

1. Phone par app **poora band** karo (recents se swipe)
2. Dobara kholo → patch download
3. **Ek baar aur** band + kholo → patch active
4. RT test: video → QC → barcode → 4 photos → save → camera turant ready hona chahiye

**Kaise**

| Item | Detail |
|------|--------|
| Log files | `{appDocuments}/RepairFully/logs/activity_YYYY-MM-DD.txt` |
| OTA target | Release `2.0.0+8` + patch `1` |
| Native warning | Patch publish hua with `--allow-native-diffs` (MainActivity drift vs release build) — Dart fixes phone tak pohanch jayenge |

Settings → **Check for updates** se bhi verify kar sakte ho.

{"contents":"import '../models/capture_session.dart';\n\n/// Pure utility — all filename patterns in one place.\n/// Never does I/O, never has side effects.\nclass FileNamingService {\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_PK.mp4\"\n  static String videoFileName(String orderId, CaptureMode mode) {\n    return '${orderId}_${mode.name.toUpperCase()}.mp4';\n  }\n\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_PK_front.jpg\"\n  static String photoFileName(String orderId, CaptureMode mode, PhotoSide side) {\n    return '${orderId}_${mode.name.toUpperCase()}_${side.name}.jpg';\n  }\n\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_meta.json\"\n  static String metaFileName(String orderId) {\n    return '${orderId}_meta.json';\n  }\n\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_compare.jpg\" (Phase 3)\n  static String compareFileName(String orderId) {\n    return '${orderId}_compare.jpg';\n  }\n\n  /// Folder name = order ID + mode suffix so PK and RT for the same order\n  /// never collide (e.g. `407-1234567-1234567-RT`).\n  static String orderFolderName(String orderId, CaptureMode mode) {\n    final safe = orderId.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\\w\\-.]'), '_');\n    return '$safe-${mode.name.toUpperCase()}';\n  }\n\n  /// Bare Amazon order ID stripped from a storage folder name.\n  static String bareOrderIdFromFolder(String folderName) {\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-PK')) {\n      return folderName.substring(0, folderName.length - 3);\n    }\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-RT')) {\n      return folderName.substring(0, folderName.length - 3);\n    }\n    return folderName;\n  }\n\n  static CaptureMode? modeFromFolder(String folderName) {\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-PK')) return CaptureMode.pk;\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-RT')) return CaptureMode.rt;\n    return null;\n  }\n}","totalLinesInFile":49}

### Assistant — 2026-07-02 11:30

**Ho chuka**

1. **Cancel → Draft retain** — Label/QC par cancel karne par video delete nahi hota; toast: *"Saved to Drafts"*. Gallery → **Drafts** → draft kholo → **Finish save** (Order ID + AWB + missing photos capture)
2. **Frame fix** — Video start par frame ab shift nahi karega (same crop preview); top aspect strip + frame-change snackbar hata diye
3. **Firebase (code analysis)** — Console direct access nahi; code se likely cause: **recording < 1 sec ya < 50KB** → error toast, label screen skip, seedha camera. Ab `stop_recording_too_short` / `stop_recording_failed` Crashlytics mein log honge

**Ab kya (Sir)**

1. App **2× band** karo → OTA patch **#2** (`2.0.0+8`) pull hoga  
2. Test: PK video stop → label par **Cancel** → Gallery **Drafts** mein video dikhe  
3. Draft → **Finish save** → barcode + photos  
4. Agar dubara stop par error aaye → Firebase Crashlytics mein `stop_recording_too_short` dekho (video kam se kam **1 second** record karo)

**Kaise**

| Flow | Path |
|------|------|
| Draft save | Gallery → Drafts → **Finish save** |
| OTA | `2.0.0+8` patch **#2** published |
| Git | `835d27f` pushed |

Firebase console link chahiye to batao — filter: `reason = stop_recording_too_short` last 1 hour.

{"contents":"import '../models/capture_session.dart';\n\n/// Pure utility — all filename patterns in one place.\n/// Never does I/O, never has side effects.\nclass FileNamingService {\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_PK.mp4\"\n  static String videoFileName(String orderId, CaptureMode mode) {\n    return '${orderId}_${mode.name.toUpperCase()}.mp4';\n  }\n\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_PK_front.jpg\"\n  static String photoFileName(String orderId, CaptureMode mode, PhotoSide side) {\n    return '${orderId}_${mode.name.toUpperCase()}_${side.name}.jpg';\n  }\n\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_meta.json\"\n  static String metaFileName(String orderId) {\n    return '${orderId}_meta.json';\n  }\n\n  /// e.g. \"407-1234567-1234567_compare.jpg\" (Phase 3)\n  static String compareFileName(String orderId) {\n    return '${orderId}_compare.jpg';\n  }\n\n  /// Folder name = order ID + mode suffix so PK and RT for the same order\n  /// never collide (e.g. `407-1234567-1234567-RT`).\n  static String orderFolderName(String orderId, CaptureMode mode) {\n    final safe = orderId.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\\w\\-.]'), '_');\n    return '$safe-${mode.name.toUpperCase()}';\n  }\n\n  /// Bare Amazon order ID stripped from a storage folder name.\n  static String bareOrderIdFromFolder(String folderName) {\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-PK')) {\n      return folderName.substring(0, folderName.length - 3);\n    }\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-RT')) {\n      return folderName.substring(0, folderName.length - 3);\n    }\n    return folderName;\n  }\n\n  static CaptureMode? modeFromFolder(String folderName) {\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-PK')) return CaptureMode.pk;\n    if (folderName.endsWith('-RT')) return CaptureMode.rt;\n    return null;\n  }\n}","totalLinesInFile":49}

{"contents":"  }\n\n  Future<void> _deleteOrder(Map<String, dynamic> order) async {\n    final id = order['orderId'] as String;\n    final ok = await _confirmDelete('order $id');\n    if (!ok) return;\n    final deleted = await _storage.deleteOrder(order['orderId'] as String);\n    if (deleted && mounted) {\n      // Push a fresh status emit so the home banner reflects the now-smaller\n      // queue immediately (deleteOrder already dropped the queue entry).\n      await SyncManager.refreshStatus();\n      _reload();\n      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(\n        content: Text('Order deleted'),\n        duration: Duration(seconds: 2),\n        backgroundColor: Colors.black87,\n      ));\n    }\n  }\n\n  /// Delete an entire draft session — every video + every photo within\n  /// the same time window. Matches the \"delete order\" semantics so users\n  /// don't have to delete photos one-by-one after deleting the video.\n  Future<void> _deleteDraftSession(Map<String, dynamic> session) async {\n    final mode = session['mode'] as String;\n    final paths = (session['draftPaths'] as List).cast<String>();\n    final n = paths.length;\n    final ok = await _confirmDelete('$mode draft session ($n file${n == 1 ? '' : 's'})');\n    if (!ok) return;\n    int deleted = 0;\n    for (final p in paths) {\n      if (await _storage.deleteDraft(p)) deleted++;\n    }\n    if (mounted) {\n      _reload();\n      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(\n        content: Text('Deleted $deleted file${deleted == 1 ? '' : 's'}'),\n        duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),\n        backgroundColor: Colors.black87,\n      ));","totalLinesInFile":2479}

{"contents":"#requires -Version 5.1\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n  RF Logger -> Shorebird ship automation. Ek hi command for \"fixes\".\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n  Patch vs release ka decision automatic leta hai aur Shorebird se ship karta\n  hai, ek stable icon-font strategy ke saath taaki patches dobara silently\n  fail na ho.\n\n  WHY --no-tree-shake-icons EVERYWHERE (permanent fix):\n    Flutter MaterialIcons-Regular.otf ko tree-shake karke sirf wahi icons\n    rakhta hai jo Dart code use karta hai. Agar patch ka icon-set release se\n    alag hua, to font asset alag ban jaata hai -> Shorebird use reject kar deta\n    hai (UnpatchableChangeException / asset diff) aur patch phone tak kabhi\n    nahi pohanchta. FULL font ko release AUR patch dono me force karne se yeh\n    drift hamesha ke liye khatam ho jaata hai.\n\n  GOLDEN RULE:\n    Patch tabhi apply hota hai jab phone par install RELEASE version EXACTLY\n    patch ke --release-version se match kare. Isse kabhi guess mat karo.\n\n.PARAMETER Mode\n  patch   : OTA Dart-only fix current/active release line ko.\n  release : +build bump, fresh Shorebird release APK (full reinstall chahiye).\n  auto    : git changes dekho; native/asset/dep change -> release, warna patch.\n\n.PARAMETER ReleaseVersion\n  Patch target override (e.g. 2.0.0+7). Default: newest active release.\n\n.PARAMETER DryRun\n  Build/validate karega par upload NAHI karega (pipeline test ke liye safe).\n\n.PARAMETER Changelog\n  Optional \"what's new\" text. UpdateService.latestChangelog ko\n  <release>:<patch> marker ke saath set karta hai aur flutter analyze se verify.\n\n.PARAMETER AllowAssetDiffs\n  Escape hatch: legacy release (jo --no-tree-shake-icons ke bina bana tha) ko\n  patch karte waqt asset diff allow karna ho to. Normally zaroorat nahi.","totalLinesInFile":347}
